### A Brand New World

| name | a\_brand\_new\_world |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | A Brand New World |
| description | The world evolves, and those who fail to adapt are forgotten by history. Our Order has endured for over half a millennium, and we will not fade away now! Our mortal bodies will eventually join the Creator, but our Order must first become immortal. ### A Central Mediterranean Power

| --- | --- |
| display\_name | A Central Mediterranean Power |
| description | Our advantageous central position in the Mediterranean enables effortless access to any shore. ### A Sheep Surrounded by Wolves

| name | hum\_a\_sheep\_surrounded\_by\_wolves |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | A Sheep Surrounded by Wolves |
| description | We exist geographically wedged between the soaring ambitions of more powerful enemies. To survive this predatory encirclement, our lords have mastered the art of pitting our neighbors against each other, wherein we perpetually offer vassalage to the distant wolf to deter the one at our doorstep. By playing the Catholic West against the Orthodox East and the Bosnian Krstjani against both, we ensure that no single hegemon can fully digest our lands without triggering a massive intervention from their rivals. This culture of sophisticated political duplicity transforms our perceived weakness into a survival mechanism, as we grant trade concessions and military access to whichever power currently guarantees our internal autonomy, effectively keeping the greater wolves at each other's throats while we remain the undisturbed masters of the Neretva. |
| icon | Advance diplomatic influence.png |
| age | Traditions |
| ai\_preference\_tags |  |
| ai\_weight |  |
| allow |  |
| allow\_children | 1 |
| content\_priority | 0 |
| country\_type |  |
| depth |  |

### Accademia del Cimento

| name | accademia\_cimento |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Accademia del Cimento |
| description | Our Grand Duke has established the *Accademia del Cimento* — the Academy of Experiment — gathering the finest scientific minds of the age under a single roof devoted to empirical inquiry, its members bound by the motto *Provando e Riprovando* — testing and retesting — that sets a new standard for the pursuit of knowledge. At the same time, the Grand Duchy is reshaping the municipalities of Tuscany, replacing the patchwork of local privileges with uniform administrative procedures that make the state's authority felt in every town and valley. This combination of scientific ambition and administrative rigor defines our duchy as a state where both ideas and governance are held to the same exacting standard of proof. |

| name | nax\_archipelagic\_vigil\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Achipelagic Vigil |
| description | The scattered islands of the Aegean demand constant vigilance from their Latin rulers, who maintain fleets of light galleys to patrol against pirates, raiders, and rival Italian maritime powers. The dukes of Naxos and their vassals have developed a sophisticated system of island garrisons, signal fires, and swift courier vessels that allow them to respond rapidly to threats across the archipelago, while the wealth extracted from trade protection and harbor dues enables the construction of sturdy coastal fortresses that make the Cyclades one of the most secure regions of the Frankokratia, a maritime bulwark. |

| description | By securing the ports of Valona and Butrint, Epirus sits as the warden of the Adriatic Sea. We have leveraged this position to negotiate favorable trade treaties with Venice and the Italian city-states, who must pass through our sphere of influence to reach the Levant. By levying precise tariffs and providing 'protection' services against corsairs, we have transformed our once-isolated despotate into a wealthy commercial hub that bridges the gap between East and West. |
| icon | Advance global trade routes advance.png |

### Adriatic Oak Galleys

| name | dal\_adriatic\_oak\_galleys |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Adriatic Oak Galleys |
| description | Recognizing that the Mediterranean is becoming a sea of fire, we have commissioned a new class of heavy, oak-reinforced oared vessels built in our best shipyards. By utilizing the iron-hard timber of the coastal mountains, these ships can withstand heavier broadsides and are designed to navigate the shallow, rocky karst inlets where large Venetian galleys founder. This superiority allows us to police our own waters and defend our coasts from any enemy. |

### Aegean Coastal Defense

| name | greek\_group\_aegean\_coastal\_defence\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Aegean Coastal Defense |
| description | The jagged coastlines and thousands of islands of the Aegean are our natural fortifications. We have transformed our maritime culture into a defensive doctrine, where every merchant vessel is armed and every fisherman is a potential marine. By patrolling the narrow straits and hidden coves, we ensure that any hostile fleet attempting to move through our waters is subjected to constant harassment. This decentralized naval power makes our maritime heartland nearly impossible to occupy. |

| --- | --- |

| description | Following the trends of the late Baroque, every major Romagnol town will establish an *Académia* focused on improving agricultural yields. ### Al Téri dl'Esarchét

| name | romagnol\_al\_teri\_dlesarchet |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Al Téri dl'Esarchét |
| description | Romagna has preserved a unique legal artifact of the Byzantine Exarchate of Ravenna. Unlike the Lombard-influenced north, Romagnol culture has retained a deeper layer of Roman-Byzantine administrative law. This has fostered a differentiated culture, where even the smallest towns claim ancient imperial privileges. This administrative complexity makes the region a nightmare for large empires to centralize efficiently, as every valley is protected by its own Roman web of exemptions and local statutes. |

### Alpine Defense

| name | aqu\_alpine\_defense |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Alpine Defense |
| description | Our territory sits at the junction of the Julian Alps and the Karst plateau, a landscape that provides natural defensive advantages to those who know how to use it and proves lethal to armies that do not. We invest in the fortification of the key passes and valley approaches, training our garrison forces to hold terrain rather than meet the enemy in open ground, and establishing a network of signal stations that ensures no advance can reach us without warning. This system of *Difesa Alpina* makes our territory one of the most costly to invade in the region, converting the rugged landscape into an ally that inflicts losses on any hostile force before our own soldiers need to engage. |
| icon | Advance defensive mentality.png |

### Alpine Diplomacy

| name | rhaetian\_the\_alpine\_diplomacy |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Alpine Diplomacy |
| description | We control the strategic mountain passes that cross the Alps. This tends to attract unwanted attention from other nations who want that same control. Out of this chaos, a specialized class of Rhaeto-Romance diplomats may emerge. Under our sponsorship, these multilingual negotiators will master the art of playing major powers against each other to maintain local autonomy. This culture of high-stakes mediation will ensure that even when foreign armies occupy the valleys, the legal and social sovereignty of the communes remains intact through meticulously drafted treaties. |

### Alpine Infantry

| name | vivaro\_alpine\_infantry |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Alpine Infantry |
| description | The mountain terrain has bred a tradition of light, mobile foot soldiers who could fight in conditions that make cavalry and heavy armor useless — men who need no road, no level ground, and no supply line longer than their own backs can carry. The valleys of our culture produce soldiers accustomed to cold, altitude, and sudden ambush, capable of holding narrow passes against forces many times their number. This tradition of alpine warfare gives our armies a quality in difficult terrain that opponents trained on the open lowland battlefield are consistently unprepared for. |

### Alpine Nature

| name | vivaro\_alpine\_nature |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Alpine Nature |
| description | Rousseau's idealization of mountain simplicity, followed by the poets and painters of the Romantic movement, transformed the Alps from a barrier to be crossed into a destination to be sought. Our valleys, peaks, and ancient communities became mirrors for everything that educated Europe felt it had lost, drawing visitors, naturalists, and artists in numbers that the passes had never seen for any other reason. This reputation for embodying a kind of unspoiled authenticity gives our culture an influence far beyond what our political weight could command — people come to us to understand themselves, and they carry home memories that outlast any treaty. |
| icon | Advance cosmopolitan court.png |

### Angevin Legitimacy

| name | alb\_angevin\_legitimacy |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Angevin Legitimacy |
| description | Our latest crowned heads can be traced directly to the lineage of the Capetian House of Anjou, specifically established by Charles I of Naples to serve as his gateway into the Byzantine heartlands. This connection grants us a unique Western prestige among the fragmented Balkan lords, allowing us to claim a seat at the table of the great Catholic monarchs of Europe. | description | Capitalizing on our position as a safe harbor, the merchant houses of Nauplia have developed a sophisticated system of maritime insurance and risk-sharing for Aegean trade. | --- | --- |
| display\_name | Aristocracy |
| description | Aristocracy is a key part of any monarchy, as the nobles surrounding the royal dynasty are precisely what gives justification to the monarch. ### Armatole Administration

| name | ths\_armatole\_administration\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Armatole Administration |
| description | To combat the lawlessness of the borderlands and the rise of banditry, the Thessalian state institutionalized the *Armatoloi*, a system where local leaders are given official military jurisdiction over specific districts. This administrative layer bridges the gap between the central Despot and the rural peasantry, creating a disciplined paramilitary class responsible for collecting taxes and maintaining internal security. By turning former outlaws into state-sanctioned peacekeepers, the despotate will achieve a level of domestic stability that will allow for renewed economic growth and a more centralized form of governance. |

### Armed Envoys

| name | army\_country\_diplo\_range\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Armed Envoys |
| description | Our army is feared far and wide. ### Arvanite Communities

| name | epi\_arvanite\_communities\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Arvanite Communities |
| description | To bolster our dwindling numbers, we have invited the fierce Arvanite clans to settle in the fertile valleys of Epirus and Acarnania. In exchange for land and autonomy, these warriors provide us with a tireless light infantry force that excels in the brutal, close-quarters combat of the Balkan frontier. Their unique martial culture, blended with our Roman military tradition, creates a hybrid force that is both exceptionally loyal and terrifyingly aggressive in the shock of battle. |

### Assizes of Romania

| name | ach\_assizes\_of\_romania\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Assizes of Romania |
| description | The feudal laws of the Frankish East are the most refined in the Catholic world, having been codified to manage a complex web of Greek serfs and French lords. These *Assizes* provide a clear legal framework for land disputes, inheritance, and military obligations, ensuring that even in times of war, the social order remains unbreakable. Rigorous adherence to these laws prevents the internal fractures that destroyed the first Crusader states, providing a rock-solid foundation for governance. |
